Staging challenged by the auditors.
  • GOLD/SILVER staged 'downcycle — post-euphoria correction' and 'recognition': CONTRADICTED IN REAL TIME. Gold has risen from $4,090.5 (Aug-3) to $4,346 (Aug-5), +6.3% in two sessions, and is now flat YTD rather than -5.1%. GDX is +7.4% today on heavy volume and -2.4% YTD rather than -11.3%. The entire 'miners-vs-bullion gap' entry edge (GDX/GLD 0.205 vs 0.23) is being arbitraged away as the report is written. Either the phase is not 'downcycle' or the entry window has already closed — the two cannot both be asserted at these prices.
  • REFINING staged 'peak / euphoria': VALIDATED, not contradicted — but the play data is now wrong in the direction that matters. PARR -15.8% in one session (to +99% YTD from +135.8%), MPC -4.8%, VLO -2.1%, with WTI down ~11% in three sessions. The euphoria call is being confirmed by the tape; the report must not present $82.87/+135.8% PARR or 'all-time highs across the group' as current, because the top-tick has already broken. This is the single most time-sensitive number set in the whole scan.
  • US NATURAL GAS / LNG staged 'early' awareness: PARTIALLY CONTRADICTED. The producer leg supports 'early' (EQT -0.1%, EXE -14.2%, AR +5.9%), but the two infrastructure plays inside the same sub-cycle do not — VG is +82.3% YTD live (the report's own +91.8% figure is a near-double) and LNG is +32.8%. A sub-cycle containing a name that has nearly doubled YTD cannot be staged 'early' wholesale; the stage should be split (producers early / LNG infrastructure recognition-to-priced) or VG and LNG moved out.
  • CHINA STEEL & ALUMINIUM 'anti-involution' staged 'early': CONTRADICTED by its own listed play. NUE is $274.74, +68.4% YTD from the verified $163.11 2025-12-31 close — not the +60.2% stated (that was the Aug-3 close of $261.28) — and sits within 2% of its 52-week high of $280.11. The write-up does self-disclaim ('own the China leg, not this one, for new money'), which is honest, but carrying a +68% name two percent off its high inside an 'early' stage bucket is a labeling error that will read as an entry invitation.
  • COPPER staged 'mid-cycle / recognition' with the ATH described as a past event: STALE. The finding cites a COMEX all-time high of $6.71/lb set May-13-2026 and a live price of $6.63. Copper is $6.7135 right now — at or fractionally above the claimed all-time high, +18.2% YTD from the verified $5.682 2025-12-31 close, not +16.8%. The metal is making new highs concurrent with the report, which argues the awareness stage is further along than 'mid-cycle/recognition' for the metal itself even if COPX (-21% off its high) still lags.
  • CROSS-CUTTING DATA-FRESHNESS DEFECT: the scan is stamped 'as_of 2026-08-04' but a large share of its live prices are Aug-3 closes (PARR $82.87, NUE $261.28, gold ~$4,119, GDX $76.05, copper $6.63), while WTI at $81.49 does not match ANY recent settlement (Aug-3 $80.34, Aug-4 $75.77, Aug-5 $75.22) and appears older still. Every awareness-stage call keyed to these levels — most acutely refining 'euphoria' and gold 'downcycle' — is being evaluated against a tape that has already moved 5-16% in the affected names. Re-pull the entire price layer before any stage label is published.
  • US short-cycle manufacturing & factory-automation — staged 'early-upcycle' / awareness 'recognition', but the headline play CGNX is a VERIFIED +96.5% YTD (35.98 → 70.71), sits ~3% below its 52-week high, is +104% off its 52-week low, and trades 38% above its 200-day average. A near-double in seven months is terminal-stage pricing, not 'early-upcycle'. The finding contradicts itself: CGNX's own valuation note concedes it is 'already priced for a complete cycle recovery' at 42.3x EV/EBITDA, which is incompatible with the sub-cycle's stage label. The group is genuinely mid-recognition (ROK +15.7%, EMR +22.4%, KMT +20.2%), so the fix is to demote CGNX to 'priced' rather than restage the whole sub-cycle. The sub-cycle's own A3 robot-order evidence (-0.1% units, auto OEM -35.1%) is also the weakest leg and is what keeps the label defensible at all.
  • Container liner & charter-owner — staged 'late-cycle / pre-downcycle' with awareness 'recognition', but verified price action says the downcycle is NOT recognized. MATX is a VERIFIED +66.3% YTD (123.55 → 205.51), and four of five plays are up 21-66% YTD (DAC +47.2%, ZIM +25.2%, GSL +21.8%). ZIM is the critical tell: it is described as 'the most spot-exposed listed liner and the segment's cleanest downcycle instrument', yet it is +25.2% YTD and trades at $26.58 against a 52-week high of $29.97 — a market that had recognized a record 38.3% orderbook and a 2027-28 delivery wave would not bid the spot-exposed liner to within 11% of its high. This is a mislabel in the direction of UNDERSTATING remaining asymmetry: the awareness stage is closer to 'latent/early' than 'recognition', which if correct means the short/avoid side of this sub-cycle still has runway. Only CMRE (-7.0%) prices any of it.
  • US truckload freight — staged 'early-upcycle (transitioning to mid-cycle)' with awareness 'recognition', but every play has already made a large move: CVLG +62.3%, HTLD +39.0%, SNDR +37.7%, KNX a VERIFIED +35.4% (52.28 → 70.81, now 17% above its 200-day and +83% off its 52-week low). 'Early-upcycle' is generous for an equity leg that has already re-rated this far. The sub-cycle's own evidence undercuts the label from the supply side too — Class 8 net orders +159% YoY in Feb-2026 and the first net INFLOW of FMCSA operating authorities since Q2-2025 mean capacity is already responding, which historically caps the rate cycle. Note also that the verified DAT data was DECLINING week-on-week at the measurement date (spot -2.5% w/w, L/T ratio -4.4% w/w) even as the YoY comparisons stayed strong; the finding cites only the YoY leg.
  • Non-corroborating check (no contradiction, recorded for balance): the two 'early'-staged sub-cycles that would matter most for alpha are the two where price action ACTUALLY supports the stage. US non-residential construction is staged 'early' and its plays are flat-to-down (VMC +0.5%, MLM -11.1%, KNF -3.6%, EXP +5.6%) against a verified -30.0% peak-to-trough drawdown in manufacturing construction and a verified -3.2% y/y June spending print. The digital-asset cycle is staged 'early' with everything down hard against a verified -48.96% BTC drawdown. These two stage calls survive adversarial price checking; the three flagged above do not.
  • SYSTEMATIC AS-OF ERROR (affects every price figure in all four segments): all quoted YTD/1Y/drawdown numbers are stamped 'as_of 2026-08-04' but actually reproduce off the 2026-08-03 close. Verified exactly on three independent tickers: SPY stated +11.1% = Aug-3 close 757.67 vs 681.92 (Aug-4 was +12.9%); GLD stated -6.2% = Aug-3 close 371.71 vs 396.31; EWY stated +64.8% = Aug-3 close 160.24 vs 97.22. Aug-4 and Aug-5 were outsized sessions (SPY +1.80% Aug-4; EWY +6.80% Aug-4; GLD +4.14% Aug-5; BKNG +6.56% Aug-5; BWA +5.93% Aug-5), so multiple early-vs-priced calls are being made on a tape two sessions stale in a fast-moving window. Every stage call anchored to a price level should be re-derived before publication.
  • EM-vs-DM staged 'early-upcycle / recognition' — CONTRADICTED by verified price action. EWY, named as 'the leg's leadership', is +74.0% YTD and +132% over 1 year (not the stated +64.8% / +121%). A +132% one-year move in the leadership vehicle is euphoria-stage price action, not recognition. The finding's own play-level note concedes '+121% 1Y = euphoria-stage pricing', which directly contradicts the sub-cycle label above it. Because Korea is also identified as the dominant driver of the EEM-over-SPY spread being used as the cycle EVIDENCE, the understated YTD/1Y figures make the 'recognition' stage look materially more defensible than the tape supports. Recommend downgrading this sub-cycle to recognition-to-euphoria, or splitting Korea out from the EM aggregate.
  • Discretionary-vs-staples staged phase 'trough' / awareness 'early' — NOT supported by absolute price levels. XLY closed 118.64 against a 52-week high of 125.01 (only -5.1% off) and RSPD, named as 'the clean mean-reversion instrument', closed 58.21 against a 60.34 high (-3.5% off) and sits ABOVE its 200dma of 56.18. RSPD is also +2.1% YTD, not the stated +0.7%. Nothing here is priced at a trough. The 'trough' label appears to describe the discretionary/staples RATIO rather than absolute price — if so the report must say so explicitly, otherwise the stage call is unsupported by the instruments actually being recommended.
  • Consumer-credit cycle staged 'early-upcycle / recognition' while the named awareness markers are already extended. ECPG is +70.5% YTD (not +77.1%) at 92.69 against a 52-week low of 36.24 — roughly a 2.5x move off the low — and BFH sits at 112.34 against a 52-week low of 53.83, near its 52-week high of 114.12. With the markers up 70-100%+, the residual asymmetry claimed for SYF/COF/OMF depends on the healing being under-recognised in those SPECIFIC names, not in the sub-cycle as a whole. The evidence presented does not establish that distinction.
  • Europe rearmament staged 'early-upcycle / recognition' with the equity leg described as 'mid-washout' / 'post-euphoria washout' — CONTRADICTED for two of the four plays. EUAD closed 47.06 against a 52-week high of 48.429 (-2.8% off) and trades above both its 50dma (42.04) and 200dma (43.02); SIEGY at 165.51 is essentially AT its 52-week high of 166.23 (the finding concedes 'the infra leg never corrected'). Only RNMBY is genuinely washed out, and even there the drawdown is overstated: -23.5% YTD (vs 365.50 on 2025-12-31), not -25.5%, and -40.4% off the 468.90 high, not -42%. A sector-level 'washout' framing is contradicted by the diversified instrument sitting at its highs.
  • Travel & experiences staged 'priced' — the stage call itself is reasonable, but the specific laggard/entry case for BKNG is not. It rested on '-10.0% YTD' and '27% off its 52-week high'; verified values are -3.4% YTD and -10.7% off the high, and the 27% figure is wrong at every point in the stated window. Separately, the CRE office sub-cycle is staged 'credit losses peaking' while the verified Trepp series shows office CMBS delinquency ROSE 4bp to 11.57% in June 2026 — fundamentals have inflected but the credit metric has not yet rolled over, so 'peaking' is a forecast being presented as observed evidence.
  • DATA-QUALITY FLAGS (not stage calls, but they corrupt drawdown math): (1) FMP's 52-week-high field for EWY reads 220.89, unsupported by any daily close in the series (max close 205.00 on 2026-06-25) — an apparent bad tick; any EWY drawdown claim built on it will be wrong by ~8pp. (2) FMP's GELYY quote carries a timestamp of 2025-08-29 on 1,226 shares of volume — the OTC line is stale/illiquid, so the stated GELYY +40.3% YTD / +65.1% 1Y could not be independently re-derived and should be re-sourced from the Hong Kong listing (0175.HK) before publication.
  • CONTRADICTED - Wide-bandgap power (SiC/GaN) staged 'early'. This is the most serious stage error in the set and it is internally self-refuting. Verified YTD: AEHR +389.3%, STM +103.4%, NVTS +73.0%, WOLF +44.7%, XFAB +31.2% - a median play up ~+73% in seven months, with the equipment read up nearly 5x. The finding's own AEHR valuation_note concedes the name is 'listed here as evidence that the equipment leg is already crowded, not as an early entry', and its NVTS note says the stock is 'priced as an AI-power option' - both statements contradict the sub_cycle-level awareness_stage of 'early'. The root confusion is conflating FUNDAMENTAL cycle position (genuinely trough-forming, per verified Yole ~50%/~70% utilisation and Wolfspeed -19% YoY) with MARKET awareness (already repriced hard off the AI-power narrative). Two different narratives are being scored under one label: 'wide-bandgap = AI power' is at euphoria; 'wide-bandgap = EV recovery' is arguably early. As written, the 'early' stamp would send a retail reader into names that have already run 73-389%.
  • CONTRADICTED - Mature-node foundry staged 'euphoria', but the tape says post-euphoria/distribution, one stage further along. Euphoria is the stage where price is still making new highs. Verified drawdowns from 52-week highs as of the 2026-08-04 close: GFS -46.6% (92.55 to 49.46), TSEM -34.0% (319.94 to 211.14), UMC -33.4% (28.96 to 19.29), VIS -31.6% (223 to 152.50), SMIC -28.3% (93.50 to 67.00). Every single play is 28-47% below its high and every one is below its 50-day moving average. The fundamental evidence (UMC guiding >90% utilisation, TrendForce +5-15% pricing, a third hike round) is all verified and still improving - which is the classic late-cycle divergence of peaking fundamentals against already-rolling price. The correct call is that euphoria has already broken, not that it is in progress; this distinction changes the action from 'avoid entering' to 'the de-rating is underway'.
  • BORDERLINE-LATE - Broad analog/MCU/passive staged 'recognition' with phase 'mid-cycle, year 2 of a 3-4y restock'. Verified median play YTD is ~+73% (TXN +60.1%, MCHP +22.1%, VSH +129.0%, DIOD +73.5%, LFUS +82.3%). A cohort where the median name has risen ~73% in seven months and one name has more than doubled is at the late edge of recognition, not mid. The finding's own phase text hedges with 'first signs of expectation overshoot' and the verified ECIA Industry Pulse reversal (154.1 June, sliding 12.5 points in July) supports a rollover, yet the stage label does not reflect it. Defensible but should be read as late-recognition.
  • DEFENSIBLE - Automotive staged 'early'. This is the best-supported stage call in the set. NXPI at a verified +6.5% YTD and -32.0% from its high is genuinely un-repriced, and the verified ADI Industrial +56% vs Automotive +2% split is strong evidence the auto leg has not yet inflected. Caveat: not all plays are early - ALGM is +58.5% YTD on a stated 76x TTM EV/EBITDA, and Sensata is +43.0%. Only NXPI and Melexis (+24.4%) genuinely carry the 'early' description.
  • CROSS-CUTTING REGIME FLAG (unacknowledged anywhere in the findings) - the entire ex-AI semi complex sold off hard in the single session these numbers were struck from. On 2026-08-04, all 16 names checked closed lower: VSH -14.6%, TSEM -11.9%, AEHR -10.1%, WOLF -6.9%, UMC -6.5%, GFS -4.9%, ON -4.8%, MCHP -3.6%, DIOD -3.1%, NXPI -2.7%, STM -2.7%, TXN -2.1%. Every one of the 16 also sits below its 50-day moving average. The findings read as though written into a rising tape and contain no drawdown or momentum context at the sub_cycle level - all the bearish price information is buried in individual valuation_note fields rather than surfaced in the phase/awareness calls. Any awareness-stage conclusion drawn from this snapshot should carry an explicit note that it was struck one day into a broad sector unwind.
  • DATA-VINTAGE FLAG - two of three CORRECTED items are staleness rather than error, and both run in the same direction (making the picture look better/earlier than it is): SMIC YTD is -6.2% not -4.8%, and VIS YTD is +65.6% not +70.5%, because HK and Taiwan closes had rolled forward one session past the prices used. Separately, the S&P Global Mobility auto-production evidence is a January-2026 vintage presented with an as_of of 2026-07, with regional revisions carrying the wrong sign for North America, Europe and Japan. Auto-cycle stage conclusions resting on that evidence block should be re-derived from the current vintage.
The audit changed this report. The independent auditors caught a systematic data-vintage defect: figures stamped as of 2026-08-04 largely reproduced the 2026-08-03 close, and several moved materially in the sessions that followed. Gold rallied 6.3% in two sessions, taking GLD from −6.2% to −1.7% year-to-date and closing most of the miners-versus-bullion gap one sub-cycle was built on; WTI broke roughly 11%; PARR fell 15.8% in a single session. Twenty-six numeric corrections and five staging corrections were applied to the underlying data before this report was rendered, and both are listed below. Four awareness stages were moved against the original analysis where verified price action contradicted it — wide-bandgap power and China anti-involution from early to recognition, EM-versus-DM to euphoria, and discretionary-versus-staples off its trough call.<br><br>How to read the sourcing. Prices and year-to-date moves were computed from the final 2025 close rather than a 52-week range, which is the most common way these numbers go wrong. Anything that could not be independently confirmed is marked (est.) at the point of use rather than being quietly dropped. The scan's blind spots are listed in the next section, and they are real.
13

What would make this wrong

top

What would make this wrong, stated before the outcome is known.

Open questions
  • Does the July China consumption plan transmit into consumer-goods PPI by mid-2027, or is the trough secular? This is the single largest swing factor in the top-ranked cycle and it is genuinely unresolved.
  • Does the US natural gas storage surplus flip to deficit through 2027, or is the EIA right that record associated gas keeps Henry Hub below $3.50? The entire second-ranked cycle turns on this one series.
  • Is the automotive semiconductor restock real end demand, or Tier-1 hedge inventory built after the 2025 Nexperia export shock? NXP's channel weeks answer this in public, quarterly.
  • Does the enhanced ACA premium tax credit get restored before the 2027 open-enrollment window? A binary, dated, poorly-modelled catalyst sitting two days before the midterms.
  • How much of the 2026 rotation into non-AI cycles is fundamental, and how much is AI-de-risking flow that reverses the moment AI leadership resumes? The healthcare segment shows this contamination most clearly.
Known limits of this scan
  • Cycle phase is only identifiable in hindsight. Every trough call here is probabilistic, and a cycle that looks early can extend for years — grower austerity buying crop protection a second year of “bottom” is the live example.
  • Staging is judgement, disciplined by price. Awareness stages were cross-checked against verified moves and valuation, but reasonable people will disagree on individual placements, which is why the reasoning is shown rather than just the label.
  • The AI exclusion is imperfect. Several segments carry AI demand that cannot be cleanly separated from public data — roughly 25-40% of the semis-ex-AI segment's growth, and essentially all US commercial construction growth. Those estimates are stated per segment and are the weakest quantitative link in the scan.
  • Two cycles are counted twice, and it inflates both. The independent MECE audit found that European rearmament appears in the macro overlay (as the fiscal turn) and again in industrials (as defence procurement), driven by the same NATO spending path with Rheinmetall listed under both; and that uranium mine-supply and utility contracting are the supply and demand sides of one price cycle, sharing a physical-trust vehicle. Both pairs are left in the sweep for transparency rather than silently merged, but each pair should be read as one bet, not two.
  • The scan is not fully exhaustive. The same audit flagged that no insurance or reinsurance underwriting cycle was covered, despite the financials lane itself noting the P&C market turned soft after 33 quarters of rate increases. That is a real gap in the collective-exhaustiveness claim and is first on the list for the next quarterly run.
  • One benchmark is contaminated. The semis-ex-AI staging argues that the group 'still lags SOXX' — but SOXX's 2026 return is overwhelmingly AI compute, so it flatters an ex-AI laggard case by construction. Read that comparison as directional only.
  • No portfolio construction. Cycles are correlated — several of the highest-scoring here are the same underlying bet on Chinese demand or on the direction of real rates. This report does not net that exposure and must not be read as a diversified list.
  • Coverage is bounded. Four sub-cycles per segment, listed vehicles only, and no private or physical-commodity expressions. Anything requiring futures, physical delivery or unlisted access was outside scope by design.
Not investment advice. This is personal research for a private knowledge base, produced with AI assistance and machine-verified where possible. It is not investment advice, not a recommendation, and not a solicitation. Every name is a research mention under the project's decision operating model — none is action-grade, none carries a position size, and nothing here has been traded. Figures are point-in-time and decay; anything marked (est.) is an estimate. Cycle diagnosis is probabilistic: troughs are only identifiable afterwards, and a cycle that looks early can extend for years. Do your own work and consider your own circumstances.
